Over the alternatives: absolute loss is robust and convex but penalises small residuals relatively more and is kinked at 0. Log-Huber is more robust but not convex → local minima. Huber is the only one robust, convex and smooth.

Also: since the cause is a known loose cable, those 7 records are provably invalid and should simply be removed.

Roman Urdu

Wajah: square loss mein har point ka gradient wala zor p′(r) = 2r hai, jo bay-had barhta hai. Jo reading 100 guna ghalat hai, uska loss 10,000 guna aur zor 100 guna hota hai — to woh 7 kharab readings data ke 0.8% se bhi kam hone ke bawajood poore average par qabza kar leti hain, aur ERM θ ko un ki taraf khisaka deta hai, jis se baqi 893 sahi readings bigad jaati hain.

Tajweez: Huber: |r| ≤ α par r², uske baad α(2|r| − α). Derivative 2α par ruk jaata hai, to har kharab reading mehdood zor lagati hai chahe kitni bhi shadeed ho, jabke 893 sahi readings par wohi faidemand quadratic sulook rehta hai. α validation se, taqreeban MAD ka 1–2 guna.

Baqi par tarjeeh kyun: absolute loss robust aur convex hai magar chhote residuals par nisbatan zyada jurmana lagata hai aur 0 par kink rakhta hai. Log-Huber zyada robust hai magar convex nahi → local minima. Huber akela hai jo robust, convex aur smooth — teeno hai.

Aur: kyunki wajah ek maaloom dheeli cable hai, wo 7 records sabit shuda ghalat hain aur unko seedha nikaal dena chahiye.

M-56 marks

A genomics group has 20,000 gene-expression features and 180 samples. Ridge returns 20,000 non-zero coefficients. Why, which regularizer instead (with formula), and two benefits beyond accuracy?

Sawal ka matlab: Genomics team ke paas 20,000 gene features aur sirf 180 samples hain. Ridge saare 20,000 coefficients non-zero de deta hai. Kyun? Uski jagah kaunsa regularizer (formula ke saath), aur accuracy ke ilawa do faide?

Jawab

English

Why no zeros: the ridge derivative is 2θj, which itself tends to 0 as θj → 0. Driving a small coefficient to exactly zero gains almost nothing in penalty (0.01 → 0.00 saves only 0.0001) while always raising empirical risk. Geometrically the ℓ2 ball is round with no vertices on the axes. Ridge shrinks, it does not select.

use the LASSO : r(theta) = ||theta||_1 = |theta_1| + ... + |theta_d|

Its derivative is ±1 — constant down to zero — and the kink at 0 pins coefficients there, giving automatic feature selection. Note d/n = 20,000/180 ≈ 111, so regularization is not optional.

Two benefits: (1) interpretability — a shortlist of ~30 genes is a biologically testable hypothesis; 20,000 coefficients are unreadable. (2) cheaper deployment — a clinical assay need only measure the selected genes.

Caution: with n = 180 expect unstable selection, so repeat across resamples and report consistently selected genes.

Roman Urdu

Zero kyun nahi aate: ridge ka derivative 2θj hai, jo θj → 0 hone par khud 0 ho jaata hai. Chhote coefficient ko bilkul zero karne se penalty mein taqreeban kuch nahi bachta (0.01 → 0.00 se sirf 0.0001) magar empirical risk hamesha barh jaata hai. Geometry: ℓ2 ka ilaaqa gol hai, axes par koi kon nahi. Ridge dabaata hai, chunta nahi.

LASSO use karein : r(theta) = ||theta||_1 = |theta_1| + ... + |theta_d|

Iska derivative ±1 hai — zero tak constant — aur 0 par kink coefficients ko waheen pakad leta hai, yaani khud-kaar feature selection. Dhyan: d/n = 20,000/180 ≈ 111, to regularization lazmi hai.

Do faide: (1) samajh mein aana — ~30 genes ki chhoti list ek aisi hypothesis hai jo lab mein test ho sakti hai; 20,000 coefficients padhe hi nahi ja sakte. (2) sasta deployment — clinical test mein sirf chune hue genes naapne padenge.

Ehtiyaat: n = 180 par chunaav be-aitbaar hoga, to data dobara sample kar kar dohrayein aur wo genes report karein jo baar baar chune jaate hain.

M-66 marks

An insurance model trained on one province is deployed nationally. A second model is trained on households where neighbours' outcomes are strongly correlated. Name the technique for each and state precisely which part of i.i.d. fails.

Sawal ka matlab: Ek insurance model sirf ek soobe ke data par bana aur poore mulk mein deploy ho gaya. Doosra model aise gharon par bana jahan parosiyon ke natije ek doosre se bohat jure hue hain. Dono ke liye technique ka naam dein aur theek theek batayein i.i.d. ka kaunsa hissa tootta hai.

Jawab

English

Case 1 — national deployment: transfer learning (domain adaptation). The part that fails is "identically distributed" — different roads, weather, vehicle mix and repair costs. Task unchanged → covariate shift. Fix: fine-tune on other provinces, re-weight samples, and validate on out-of-province data only.

Case 2 — correlated neighbours: collective classification. The part that fails is independence — outcomes are spatially autocorrelated (shared flood plains, crime rates, weather), so one household's outcome informs another's. Predict linked households jointly, and split validation data by neighbourhood, not by household.

The distinction: distribution changed → transfer learning; points linked to one another → collective classification. These are the two halves of i.i.d., with different remedies.

Roman Urdu

Case 1 — poore mulk mein deployment: transfer learning (domain adaptation). Jo hissa tootta hai wo "identically distributed" hai — sadkein, mausam, gaadiyon ki qism aur marammat ke kharche sab alag. Kaam wohi hai → covariate shift. Ilaaj: doosre soobon ke data par fine-tune, samples ko dobara weight dena, aur validation sirf soobe se bahar ke data par.

Case 2 — jure hue parosi: collective classification. Jo hissa tootta hai wo independence hai — natije jagah ke lehaz se jure hue hain (ek hi nikaas, ek hi jurm ki shah, ek hi mausam), to ek ghar ka natija doosre ke baare mein bata deta hai. Jure hue gharon ka faisla mila kar karein, aur validation data mohalle ke hisaab se baantein, ghar ke hisaab se nahi.

Farq: distribution badli → transfer learning; points ek doosre se jure → collective classification. Ye i.i.d. ke do hisse hain, aur in ka ilaaj alag hai.

M-76 marks

A reservoir model predicts safe water release. Releasing too much floods downstream; too little merely wastes hydro-power. Which loss, what range for its parameter (with an explicit sign calculation), and what is the optimal constant called?

Sawal ka matlab: Dam ka model batata hai kitna paani chhodna mehfooz hai. Zyada chhod dein to neeche sailaab; kam chhodein to sirf bijli zaya. Kaunsa loss, uske parameter ki kaunsi range (nishaan ke hisaab ke saath), aur behtareen constant ko kya kehte hain?

Jawab

English

Loss: tilted absolute loss with τ < ½.

SIGN CHECK. r = y-hat - y safe volume y = 400, model predicts y-hat = 550 r = 550 - 400 = +150 (POSITIVE) -> too much released -> FLOODING => FLOODING = over-estimation = r > 0 <- dangerous side r >= 0 costs (1-tau)|r| ; r < 0 costs tau|r| need (1 - tau) > tau => tau < 1/2 tau = 0.05 : over-release by 50 -> 0.95(50) = 47.5 under-release by 50 -> 0.05(50) = 2.5 -> 19 : 1

Optimal constant: the τ-quantile — at τ = 0.05, the 5th percentile, a deliberately conservative low release so only ~5% of decisions over-release.

Sanity check: penalising over-release should make the model release less; a small τ gives a low quantile. ✓ Caveat: a quantile loss gives asymmetry, not a hard limit; for a true flood threshold add an explicit constraint.

Roman Urdu

Loss: tilted absolute loss, τ < ½ ke saath.

NISHAAN CHECK. r = y-hat - y mehfooz miqdaar y = 400, model kehta hai y-hat = 550 r = 550 - 400 = +150 (POSITIVE) -> zyada paani chhoda -> SAILAAB => SAILAAB = zyada batana = r > 0 <- khatarnak taraf r >= 0 ki qeemat (1-tau)|r| ; r < 0 ki qeemat tau|r| chahiye (1 - tau) > tau => tau < 1/2 tau = 0.05 : 50 zyada chhoda -> 0.95(50) = 47.5 50 kam chhoda -> 0.05(50) = 2.5 -> 19 : 1

Behtareen constant: τ-quantile — τ = 0.05 par 5th percentile, yaani jaan boojh kar ehtiyaat wali kam miqdaar, taake sirf ~5% faislon mein zyada paani chhoda jaaye.

Aqal se tasdeeq: zyada chhodne par saza lagane se model kam chhodega; aur chhota τ neecha quantile deta hai. ✓ Ehtiyaat: quantile loss sirf na-barabari deta hai, pakki had nahi — asli sailaab ki had ke liye alag se constraint lagayein.

M-86 marks

A bookstore recommender is built from 6 million transaction records with no target column, observing P(cookbook | kitchen gadget) = 0.55. A manager insists this is "obviously supervised learning since we are predicting purchases". Settle the disagreement.

Sawal ka matlab: Bookstore ka recommender 60 lakh transactions se bana, jin mein koi target column nahi, aur mila P(cookbook | kitchen gadget) = 0.55. Manager zid kar raha hai ke "ye zahir hai supervised learning hai, kyunki hum khareedari ka andaza lagaa rahe hain". Is ikhtilaaf ka faisla karein.

Jawab

English

As described this is association analysis — the manager is conflating "predicting something" with "supervised learning".

Not supervised: that needs labelled pairs with a designated target, and the data has no target column. The 0.55 is an association rule's confidence. Nor is it clustering: the relationships are between items, not partitions of customers.

Where the manager has a point: association analysis does produce predictions — that simply does not imply supervision. And the task could be reframed as supervised ("will this customer buy a cookbook?") with per-customer labels.

Resolution: association analysis is item-centric, label-free, and yields rules over all item pairs at once — what a catalogue-wide recommender needs, since a separate classifier per product is infeasible. Answering the question as posed: no labels exist, so association analysis.

Roman Urdu

Jaisa bataya gaya hai, ye association analysis hai — manager "kisi cheez ka andaza lagana" aur "supervised learning" ko mila raha hai.

Supervised nahi: us ke liye labelled jode aur ek muqarrar target chahiye, aur is data mein koi target column nahi. 0.55 ek association rule ka confidence hai. Aur ye clustering bhi nahi: rishte items ke darmiyan hain, customers ki taqseem nahi.

Manager ki baat mein jo sahi hai: association analysis waqai predictions deta hai — magar is se wo supervised nahi ban jaata. Aur haan, sawal ko supervised bana bhi sakte hain ("ye customer cookbook khareedega?"), magar us ke liye per-customer labels chahiye.

Faisla: association analysis items par markoz hai, usay label nahi chahiye, aur wo saari item jodiyon ke rules ek saath deta hai — jo poore catalogue ke recommender ke liye zaroori hai, kyunki har product ka alag classifier banana namumkin hai. Sawal ka jawab jaisa poocha gaya: labels mojood nahi, to association analysis.

M-96 marks

A clinic uses 1-NN over 6 measurements including creatinine (0.5–1.2 mg/dL) and systolic blood pressure (90–200 mmHg). Training accuracy is 100%, deployment is poor. Explain both defects quantitatively and state the remedies.

Sawal ka matlab: Clinic 1-NN use karti hai 6 measurements par, jin mein creatinine (0.5–1.2) aur blood pressure (90–200) shamil hain. Training par 100% accuracy, magar asal mein buri. Dono kharabiyan numbers ke saath samjhayein aur ilaaj batayein.

Jawab

English

Defect 1 — 100% training accuracy is vacuous. Each point is its own nearest neighbour (distance 0), so error is 0 for any loss — from the definition, not from learning. Why k=1 overfits: each prediction rests on one point, so no averaging suppresses label noise and one misdiagnosis corrupts its whole Voronoi cell; the predictor is a step function, hence maximally sensitive.

Defect 2 — unscaled features let blood pressure dominate:

A: creatinine 0.7, BP 110 B: creatinine 1.2, BP 170 sq. distance = (0.5)^2 + (60)^2 = 0.25 + 3600 = 3600.25 BP contributes 3600/3600.25 = 99.993 % creatinine contributes 0.25/3600.25 = 0.007 %

So "nearest patient" means, to four decimals, "closest blood pressure". A 0.5 mg/dL creatinine difference — clinically normal-to-impaired renal function — is invisible. Since 1-NN has no parameters, nothing can compensate: the distance is the model. It is also unit-dependent.

Remedies: standardize every feature (training statistics only); raise k or use soft-NN with β; select by validation, never training error; consider feature selection.

Roman Urdu

Kharabi 1 — 100% training accuracy khaali hai. Har point apna hi nearest neighbour hai (faasla 0), to error kisi bhi loss ke saath 0 — ye tareef se nikla, seekhne se nahi. k=1 overfit kyun karta hai: har jawab ek point par tika hai, to label ka shor dabane ke liye koi averaging nahi, aur ek ghalat diagnosis apne poore ilaaqe (Voronoi cell) ko kharab kar deta hai; prediction step function hai, yaani maximum sensitive.

Kharabi 2 — bagair scaling blood pressure poore faasle par qabza kar leta hai:

A: creatinine 0.7, BP 110 B: creatinine 1.2, BP 170 squared faasla = (0.5)^2 + (60)^2 = 0.25 + 3600 = 3600.25 BP ka hissa = 3600/3600.25 = 99.993 % creatinine ka hissa = 0.25/3600.25 = 0.007 %

Yaani "sab se qareeb patient" ka matlab chaar decimal tak "jiska blood pressure sab se qareeb" ban gaya. 0.5 mg/dL creatinine ka farq — jo medically guurde ke theek hone aur kharab hone ka faasla hai — nazar hi nahi aa raha. Aur kyunki 1-NN mein koi parameter nahi, is ko theek karne ka koi zariya nahi: faasla hi model hai. Ye unit par bhi depend karta hai.

Ilaaj: har feature standardize karein (sirf training ki statistics se); k barhayein ya soft-NN use karein β ke saath; chunaav validation se karein, training error se hargiz nahi; aur feature selection par ghaur karein.

M-106 marks

State the definition of a convex function and the optimality condition for a non-differentiable convex function. Then explain why these two facts make ℓ1 both computable and sparsifying.

Sawal ka matlab: Convex function ki tareef aur un shartein likhein jo non-differentiable convex function ke minimum ke liye hain. Phir samjhayein ke ye do baatein mil kar ℓ1 ko nikaalne ke qabil aur sparsity dene wala — dono kaise bana deti hain.

Jawab

English

Definition: f(αw + (1−α)z) ≤ αf(w) + (1−α)f(z) for all w, z and α ∈ [0,1] — every chord lies on or above the graph.

Optimality without differentiability: w minimises f iff f′−(w) ≤ 0 and f′+(w) ≥ 0, where the one-sided derivatives always exist for a convex function. (For |w| at 0 they are −1 and +1, so 0 is the minimiser although f′(0) does not exist.)

Why ℓ1 is computable: ‖θ‖1 is convex, so the lasso objective is convex — every local minimum is global, the conditions are sufficient, and the solution is reproducible and efficiently computed. The kink removes the closed form but convexity preserves tractability.

Why ℓ1 is sparsifying: the kink is the mechanism. The derivative is ±1 — constant right to zero, unlike ridge's vanishing 2θj — and because it jumps from −1 to +1 at the origin, both one-sided conditions can hold exactly at θj = 0 for a whole range of data gradients: whenever the risk pull is smaller than λ, the coefficient is pinned there.

Synthesis: non-differentiability creates sparsity; convexity keeps it solvable. ℓ1 is the unique power with both.

Roman Urdu

Tareef: har w, z aur α ∈ [0,1] ke liye f(αw + (1−α)z) ≤ αf(w) + (1−α)f(z) — yaani koi bhi chord curve ke oopar ya us par rehta hai.

Bagair differentiability optimality: w minimum hai agar aur sirf agar f′−(w) ≤ 0 aur f′+(w) ≥ 0, aur convex function ke ye one-sided derivatives hamesha mojood hote hain. (|w| ke liye 0 par ye −1 aur +1 hain, to 0 minimum hai halaanke f′(0) mojood nahi.)

ℓ1 nikaalne ke qabil kyun hai: ‖θ‖1 convex hai, to lasso ka objective bhi convex — har local minimum global hai, shartein kaafi hain, aur jawab dohraya ja sakta hai aur efficiently nikalta hai. Kink closed-form formula khatam kar deta hai, magar convexity hal nikaalne ki aasani barqaraar rakhti hai.

ℓ1 sparsity kyun deta hai: kink hi asal mechanism hai. Derivative ±1 hai — zero tak constant, ridge ke khatam hone wale 2θj ke barkhilaaf — aur kyunki wo origin par −1 se +1 tak chhalaang lagata hai, to dono one-sided shartein θj = 0 par hi poori ho sakti hain, aur ye data ke gradients ki poori range ke liye hota hai: jab bhi data ka zor λ se chhota ho, coefficient waheen jam jaata hai.

Khulasa: non-differentiability sparsity paida karti hai; convexity usay hal karne qabil rakhti hai. ℓ1 wo akeli power hai jis mein dono hain.

Mock ke baad — khud ko check karein

Har sawal par chaar cheezein dekhein. Chaaron hain to 6/6; sirf naam likha hai to 2/6.

  • Naam liya? Technique ka theek naam, pehli line mein.
  • Mechanism diya? Formula ya derivative — p′(r) = 2r, 2θj → 0, ρ‖θ‖1.
  • Scenario se joda? Sawal ke apne lafz aur numbers (GPS glitch, 200 features, age in years).
  • Sifarish ki? Kya karein + "choose α / λ / k by validation" — taqreeban hamesha fit hota hai.
